00244: def cloth_mesh(name,width,height,origin,phase):
00245:     verts,faces = [],[]
00246:     nx,nz = 12,17
00247:     for j in range(nz):
00248:         t = j/(nz-1)
00249:         for i in range(nx):
00250:             u = i/(nx-1)
00251:             x = origin[0]+(u-.5)*width*(.65+.35*t)
00252:             y = origin[1]+.032*math.cos(u*math.pi*6+phase)+.035*math.sin(t*3)
00253:             z = origin[2]-height*t+.025*math.sin(u*9+phase)*t**5
00254:             verts.append((x,y,z))
00255:     for j in range(nz-1):
00256:         for i in range(nx-1):
00257:             a = j*nx+i
00258:             faces.append((a,a+1,a+nx+1,a+nx))
00259:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00260:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces)
00261:     mesh.update()
00262:     ob = bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00263:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00264:     ob.data.materials.append(clothmat)
00265:     ob.parent = bpy.data.objects.get('towel_fixture')

00267:     mod.thickness = .006
00268:     for poly in mesh.polygons:
00269:         poly.use_smooth = True
00270:

00123: # A cloth surface with irregular hem and overlapping folded panels.
00124: def cloth_mesh(name, width, height, origin, fold=0):
00125:     verts=[]; faces=[]
00126:     nx,nz=12,17
00127:     for j in range(nz):
00128:         t=j/(nz-1)
00129:         for i in range(nx):
00130:             u=i/(nx-1)
00131:             x=origin[0]+(u-.5)*width*(.70+.30*t)
00132:             y=origin[1]+.027*math.cos(u*math.pi*6+fold)+.035*math.sin(t*3)
00133:             z=origin[2]-height*t+.025*math.sin(u*9+fold)*t**5
00134:             verts.append((x,y,z))
00135:     for j in range(nz-1):
00136:         for i in range(nx-1):
00137:             a=j*nx+i
00138:             faces.append((a,a+1,a+nx+1,a+nx))
00139:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00140:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces); mesh.update()
00141:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00142:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00143:     return ob
00144:

00195: def meshes_under(name):

00197:     return [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH'] if root else []

00220: def cloth_mesh(name,width,height,origin,phase):
00221:     verts=[]
00222:     faces=[]
00223:     nx,nz=12,17
00224:     for j in range(nz):
00225:         t=j/(nz-1)
00226:         for i in range(nx):
00227:             u=i/(nx-1)
00228:             x=origin[0]+(u-.5)*width*(.65+.35*t)
00229:             y=origin[1]+.032*math.cos(u*math.pi*6+phase)+.035*math.sin(t*3)
00230:             z=origin[2]-height*t+.025*math.sin(u*9+phase)*t**5
00231:             verts.append((x,y,z))
00232:     for j in range(nz-1):
00233:         for i in range(nx-1):
00234:             a=j*nx+i
00235:             faces.append((a,a+1,a+nx+1,a+nx))
00236:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00237:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces)
00238:     mesh.update()
00239:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00240:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00241:     ob.data.materials.append(clothmat)
00242:     ob.parent=bpy.data.objects.get('towel_fixture')
00243:     mod=ob.modifiers.new('Cloth thickness','SOLIDIFY')
00244:     mod.thickness=.006
00245:     for poly in mesh.polygons:
00246:         poly.use_smooth=True
00247:

00244: def cloth_mesh(name,width,height,origin,phase):
00245:     verts,faces = [],[]
00246:     nx,nz = 12,17
00247:     for j in range(nz):
00248:         t = j/(nz-1)
00249:         for i in range(nx):
00250:             u = i/(nx-1)
00251:             x = origin[0]+(u-.5)*width*(.65+.35*t)
00252:             y = origin[1]+.032*math.cos(u*math.pi*6+phase)+.035*math.sin(t*3)
00253:             z = origin[2]-height*t+.025*math.sin(u*9+phase)*t**5
00254:             verts.append((x,y,z))
00255:     for j in range(nz-1):
00256:         for i in range(nx-1):
00257:             a = j*nx+i
00258:             faces.append((a,a+1,a+nx+1,a+nx))
00259:     mesh = bpy.data.meshes.new(name)
00260:     mesh.from_pydata(verts,[],faces)
00261:     mesh.update()

00264:     ob.data.materials.append(clothmat)

00269:         poly.use_smooth = True
